## Dialogue 3: Ellsworth moment mountain bike.Large
Category: bike | List price: 1200 | Buyer target: 1104 | Seller target: 1200

- **t0 BUYER:** Hi I am interested in your bike.
- **t1 SELLER:** Great, do you have any questions?
- **t2 BUYER:** I was wondering if you are willing to take 100 less for it? Also where did you keep the bike?
- **t3 SELLER:** It is garage kept ever since I have owned it. It is top of the line. All new parts and rarely riden. I would not accept 100.00 I would work on my asking price of 1200.00 though
- **t4 BUYER:** I was asking if you would take 1100 not 100. Sorry I should have been more specific. Sounds liek a great bike, why are you selling it?
- **t5 SELLER:** I am just getting to old to ride, and I have no children. I want someone else to enjoy it. I would accept 1100 for the bike, I want someone to own it who wil use it and have fun with it.
- **t6 BUYER:** That sounds good to me and I thank you being so reasonable about it.

## Dialogue 19: NOT GETTING SIGNAL  CALL THE SATTELITE GUY

- **t6 BUYER:** I am willing to pay $29 as that fits my budget just now.

- **t8 BUYER:** I can do $32 last. Would that work?
- **t9 SELLER:** Okay, now let's set up a time for me to come over to your house to do the work.

## Dialogue 26: 2009 Motobecane FLY Pro Hardtail MTB
Category: bike | List price: 800 | Buyer target: 736 | Seller target: 800

- **t0 BUYER:** Hi - I saw your bike posted and would like more information on it please. how are the tires?
- **t1 SELLER:** Thank you for your interest. They were recently replaced and are in excellent shape.
- **t2 BUYER:** Ok - is this a 10, 12, 18 or 21 speed?
- **t3 SELLER:** This is a 21 speed.
- **t4 BUYER:** It is difficult to see from the one picture - does this have disc brakes?
- **t5 SELLER:** It has Avid Juicy 7 Hydraulic Disc Brakes
- **t6 BUYER:** would you consider anything for trade?
- **t7 SELLER:** Not at this time, but if it helps I'd be willing to deliver the bike to you and allow you to try it out.
- **t8 BUYER:** Ok, I live in Bogota, Columbia, South America, so the trip may be costly.
- **t9 SELLER:** You're in luck.  We're planning a trip to Bogota, Columbia.
- **t10 BUYER:** Wow - what luck! Ok, since the bike is 8 years old, I can offer $650 for it. Is that something you would consider?
- **t11 SELLER:** That's a serious offer, but the bike cost far more than that new and it has been maintained properly.  I'm also bringing it to you which shaves a lot off of delivery costs.  $700 sounds better to me.
- **t12 BUYER:** Can we make offer contingent upon seeing it in person, and trying it out? If it looks good, then I will pay 700
- **t13 SELLER:** That works for me.  Worst case scenario I'll use the bike on the trip.
- **t14 BUYER:** thanks alot - see you in Bogota!
- **t15 SELLER:** Thank you! See you soon.

## Dialogue 109: "FULLY REMODELED" 4 Bdrm, 1 Bath, Duplex

## Dialogue 237: 2009 Motobecane FLY Pro Hardtail MTB
Category: bike | List price: 800 | Buyer target: 560 | Seller target: 800

- **t0 BUYER:** Hey, I'm interested in this item but I'm on a budget. Is your list price your final offer?
- **t1 SELLER:** This bike is a great deal as you can see from the description.  I feel as though $800 is a reasonable asking price considering the high end components.

- **t3 SELLER:** Would you be interested if I delivered it to you for free and dropped the price to $775?

- **t6 BUYER:** That's not a very big price drop. What about $600?
- **t7 SELLER:** The lowest I will go is $700 and no other perks.
- **t8 BUYER:** That's just too high for me. Are you sure?
- **t9 SELLER:** $700 is more than fair.
- **t10 BUYER:** Can you tell me anything more about the bike that will convince me of that? All I see is a 2009 aluminum frame, the crankset, and the handlebars and stem.
- **t11 SELLER:** It has Shimano Devore XT crankset, Ritchie's Comp handlebars and stem, Avid Juicy 7 Hydrolic Disc Brakes.  These are all high end components.
- **t12 BUYER:** Ah, I didn't know about the brakes! That makes a big difference for me. I'm willing to pay $650, in cash if you'd prefer, and pick it up myself.
- **t13 SELLER:** $690 and it is yours.
- **t14 BUYER:** Are you willing to go just a bit lower, perhaps $675?
- **t15 SELLER:** Ok, it's a deal at $675!
- **t16 BUYER:** I agree! But for some reason, I can't submit the offer from my end. Are you able to do so?
- **t17 SELLER:** Yup, I'm submitting right now.
- **t19 BUYER:** I figured it out.
